Our method examines the sorted router probability d","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-05T03:10:17Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2608_04401.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"MoE模型传统固定top-k路由机制不区分token的专家需求度——部分仅需少量专家的token浪费算力，而需要更多专家的token可能被分配不足；现有动态路由方案均需要重新训练或超参数搜索，成本高且无法直接复用已训练完成的MoE checkpoint。","importance":"提出了首个无需训练、无需额外超参数的MoE动态路由插件，可直接部署于已训练完成的MoE模型，在不损失精度的前提下降低推理延迟，为MoE推理优化提供了轻量可落地的新路径。","technical_approach":"对每个token的路由概率做降序排序，使用Kneedle算法识别概率曲线的肘点（即快速衰减的头部过渡到平坦尾部的拐点），将肘点作为该token的动态激活专家数，上限为原模型的固定top-K值；该方法仅裁剪原top-K中的低概率专家，不改变专家排序或引入新路径，从信号-噪声分解角度解释：高概率头部对应与输入相关的信号专家，平坦尾部对应正交残差驱动的噪声专家，肘点即为二者的分界。","experiments":"在OLMoE模型上测试，覆盖MMLU、ARC-Easy、ARC-Challenge、HellaSwag、PIQA、WinoGrande六个基准；分析超过200万条路由概率曲线，99.7%存在清晰的尖锐肘点，且肘点分布跨数据集、跨层、跨学科主题一致；随机替换肘点后的低概率专家不影响精度，替换肘点前的专家则精度显著下降；最终实现平均5.3%的推理延迟降低，同时保持精度与专家负载平衡。","reading_guide":"重点看第2节的肘点检测算法和信号-噪声解释，图2的尾部分布随机化实验和图3的肘点分布统计；预计阅读时间15-20分钟，适合快速了解MoE推理侧的无训练优化思路。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Conventional fixed top-k routing in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models applies uniform expert allocation across all tokens, wasting compute on tokens that only need a small number of relevant experts while potentially under-provisioning tokens that require more. Existing dynamic routing methods all require retraining, auxiliary losses, or expensive hyperparameter grid search, limiting their deployability on finished pretrained MoE checkpoints.","importance":"This work introduces the first training-free, hyperparameter-free dynamic routing plugin for MoE models that can be directly applied to existing checkpoints. It delivers measurable latency reduction without accuracy loss, providing a lightweight, production-ready path for MoE inference optimization.","technical_approach":"For each token, router probabilities are sorted in descending order. A Kneedle-style algorithm detects the elbow point on the sorted probability curve — the inflection point where the rapidly decaying high-probability head transitions to a flat low-probability tail. The number of active experts for the token is set to the minimum of the elbow index and the model's original fixed top-K value. The method only prunes low-probability experts from the original top-K set, preserving expert ordering and introducing no new routing paths. A signal-noise decomposition framework explains the approach: high-probability head experts correspond to signal aligned with input-relevant routing directions, while flat tail experts reflect noise from orthogonal residual variation, with the elbow marking the boundary between the two.","experiments":"Evaluations are conducted on the OLMoE model across six benchmarks: MMLU, ARC-Easy, ARC-Challenge, HellaSwag, PIQA, and WinoGrande. Analysis of over 2 million router probability curves shows 99.7% have clear, sharp elbows, with elbow distributions consistent across datasets, router layers, and MMLU subject categories. Randomly replacing post-elbow low-probability experts has no measurable impact on accuracy, while replacing pre-elbow experts causes significant accuracy drops. The method achieves an average 5.3% inference latency reduction while preserving full benchmark accuracy and expert load balance.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 2 for the elbow detection algorithm and signal-noise interpretation, Figure 2 for the tail randomization validation experiment, and Figure 3 for elbow distribution statistics. inference backends (e.g., HuggingFace Transformers, vLLM, Ollama) as transparent, interchangeable components, and rarely disclose framework names, versions, or configuration details. No systematic study has previously quantified how much backend choice alone alters model outputs and benchmark scores, leaving an underrecognized threat to result reproducibility and conclusion validity.","importance":"This work identifies a previously underquantified confound in all LLM evaluation: even under fully deterministic greedy decoding with zero sampling randomness, swapping inference backends alone produces structural, non-noise performance differences. The finding directly challenges the reliability of small incremental performance claims, ablation results, and cross-paper model comparisons, particularly when score gaps between model variants are narrow enough to be swamped by backend effects. It carries direct implications for experimental rigor across pretraining, SFT, and DPO pipelines.","technical_approach":"$18","experiments":"Three core findings emerge: First, backend choice induces large, structural performance differences even under greedy decoding with no sampling noise, with effect sizes far exceeding statistical fluctuation. Second, effect sizes are strongly model-dependent, with different model families showing widely varying sensitivity to backend changes. Third, effects are unevenly distributed across task types: factual knowledge benchmarks show substantially larger score gaps across backends than social bias benchmarks. Layered comparison reveals differences at every level of the stack: even the thin hf_pipeline abstraction (sharing the exact same model object as the reference) introduces measurable divergence, pointing to preprocessing, default generation parameters, and output postprocessing as early sources of bias; vLLM shows larger gaps due to its independent decoding and attention implementations; Ollama shows the largest gaps, consistent with its fully independent runtime stack.","reading_guide":"For close reading, start with the experimental control design, particularly the layered backend setup that allows localizing difference sources to specific stack layers (abstraction wrappers, decoding logic, full runtime). Focus heavily on results from the deterministic greedy decoding condition, which cleanly separates structural backend effects from sampling randomness. Review the breakdown of effect sizes across task types and model families to identify scenarios where backend confounds are most likely to flip evaluation conclusions. Open questions worth following up on: whether this effect scales with model size, which specific implementation detail (chat template rendering, default logit processing, KV cache behavior, tokenizer edge cases) contributes the largest share of divergence, and what fraction of reported 0.x-point gains from SFT/DPO or small pretraining adjustments can be attributed to unaligned backend configurations.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"评测复现性的硬证据，今后内部 leaderboard 必须锁 backend+版本；只看方差分解表和 greedy 那一节。","en":"Hard evidence on eval reproducibility; pin backend+version on internal leaderboards. The target is not factual recall or multiple-choice reasoning, but long-form professional judgment where there is no single gold answer and where domain experts can still decide whether an answer is acceptable.","importance":"The main value is in evaluation design. The benchmark exposes a gap between conventional automatic benchmarks and high-stakes professional use cases. For pretraining and post-training research, it provides a cleaner signal for capabilities that are hard to measure with closed-form tasks: constraint tracking, local institutional knowledge, risk-aware reasoning, and long-form decision support. It is also relevant to data contamination concerns, because the tasks are newly authored by experts from real professional experience rather than drawn from public answer-key datasets.","technical_approach":"The benchmark contains 413 long-form tasks written by 47 vetted experts across finance, marketing, business, and product. Items are required to be self-contained, specific, goal-oriented, open-ended, and European-market-specific. Each task comes with an item-level checklist of 5 to 10 required elements, written together with the question. The dataset goes through a quality pipeline, including an automated QA stage using Claude Sonnet 4.6 to flag checklist leakage, irrelevant checklists, unsafe or non-self-contained prompts, unclear roles, unprofessional wording, and underspecified scenarios. Model responses are then evaluated by human experts with three instruments: a shared multi-attribute rubric, the item-specific checklist, and explicit preference ranking. The aggregate metric is Solve Rate. The key methodological choice is to use expert judgment as the primary evaluation signal rather than string matching or model-based judging.","experiments":"The study spends more than 4,000 expert-hours evaluating six frontier language models. The strongest model solves only 56.9% of the tasks, while blindly judged expert-written reference answers are near ceiling. The reported failure pattern is consistent with practical long-form use: models often produce plausible and well-structured answers, but miss required constraints, under-handle local European context, or fail to make the tradeoffs an expert would expect. The combination of rubric scores, checklist coverage, and preference rankings is used to reduce dependence on any single subjective score.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 to 35 minutes. Focus on the dataset construction protocol, expert selection, checklist authoring, and Solve Rate definition. The graded example in the appendix is useful because it shows what model failure looks like at the level of missing decision-critical requirements. The results section is mainly useful for understanding how far current models remain from expert references under human evaluation. 里，技能（skill）通常只以“名称+短描述”先暴露给模型，完整步骤需要模型主动检索后才能执行。现有评测多看最终任务成败或技能文档质量，无法回答一个更基础的问题：模型是否能自己判断该用哪个技能、主动触发检索、并按技能步骤执行且不越界。该论文要评测的就是这种端到端的“技能使用能力”，并把失败拆成可定位的三个环节。","importance":"对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，但对“posttrain/agent However, common approaches such as verbalization produce extremely sparse outputs. For instance, Qwen3-32B verbalizes only eight unique confidence values on SST-2, with over half being exactly 95%, a pattern we observe consistently across four datasets and two LLMs. Besides limiting practical utility, we show that this sparsity critically affects evaluation: the choice of interpo","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-05T14:24:34Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_04899.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"LLM 用于分类时常需要“置信度”来做 selective prediction（低置信度拒答/拒分）。但在黑盒场景下常用的 verbalization（让模型输出 0–100 的自报置信度）会产生极度稀疏的置信度取值，导致阈值选择空间很小；更隐蔽的是，这种稀疏会让 AUARC（accuracy–rejection curve 面积）对插值方式异常敏感，从而改变方法排名，造成不公平或不可复现的比较。","importance":"对预训练不是直接贡献，但对“评测与训练信号质量”很相关：\n1) 指出一个容易被忽略的评测实现细节（AUARC 的插值）会在稀疏置信度下主导结论，属于典型的 evaluation pitfall；\n2) 把“输出 token 偏好导致的数值稀疏”作为独立问题提出，提醒在做基于自然语言接口的概率/不确定性估计时，tokenizer 与生成偏置会直接影响可用性；\n3) 提供一个几乎零成本的改进（verbalization logprobs），把离散自报数值转成连续置信度分布，适合作为工程侧默认基线。","technical_approach":"论文比较三类黑盒/半黑盒置信度估计：\n- Verbalization：让模型输出一个数值置信度（可配 CoT/self-probing/top-k verbalization 等变体）。\n- Sampling-based：多次采样分类输出，用一致性频率或对 verbalized confidence 求均值等得到置信度。\n- Logit-based：利用 API 返回的 token logprobs（如类标签 token 的概率）作为更接近传统分类器的置信度。\n核心分析在于“稀疏性”：verbalization 输出集中在少数几个数字（由数值 token 偏好等因素导致），使得 risk–coverage 曲线只有少数拐点。\n评测陷阱：AUARC 需要对离散点做插值积分。线性插值会在点与点之间“虚构”连续变化，可能夸大某些方法；stepwise（阶梯）插值更符合 selective prediction 的阈值语义（阈值跨过某个离散置信度前，覆盖率/准确率不应连续变化）。作者主张标准化使用 stepwise。\n方法改进：verbalization logprobs。做法是仍让模型 verbalize 置信度数字，但不把生成的单个数字当最终值，而是用这些数字 token 的概率（logprobs）对可能的置信度取值加权，从而把稀疏的点估计变成连续分布/连续分数；计算上不需要额外采样，前提是能拿到生成 token 的 logprobs。","experiments":"稀疏现象：例如 Qwen3-32B 在 SST-2 上只产生 8 个不同置信度值（0.6/0.65/0.7/0.75/0.85/0.9/0.95/0.98），且超过一半样本为 95%。作者称该现象在 4 个数据集、2 个 LLM 上都一致出现。\n插值影响：在 AUARC 上，verbalization 与 sampling 方法在 stepwise 插值下相对 linear 插值下降 0.7–4.5 分；token logprobs 这类连续分数方法基本不受影响。更关键的是，某个方法在 linear 下最好、在 stepwise 下可能变成最差，说明“插值选择”足以翻转结论。\n改进效果：verbalization logprobs 在 4 个数据集上相对 vanilla verbalization 提升约 +2.3 AUARC，且无额外计算成本（不增加采样次数）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 25–35 分钟）：\n1) 先看稀疏性诊断：不同方法输出分布的统计图/表（理解问题不是校准误差，而是取值离散）。\n2) 精读 AUARC 计算细节：线性 vs 阶梯插值的定义、为什么稀疏时会改变排名（这是论文最“可迁移”的部分）。\n3) 看 verbalization logprobs 的实现：需要哪些 logprob、如何把数字 token 概率映射到连续置信度；再看与 vanilla verbalization 的对比表。\n4) 如果你在做评测或产品：把 stepwise AUARC 作为默认实现，并在报告中明确插值方式；同时报告置信度取值的 unique count/熵，避免只给 AUARC 一个数字。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"When LLMs are used as classifiers, confidence estimates are needed for selective prediction (rejecting low-confidence cases). Popular black-box approaches like confidence verbalization (asking the model to output a 0–100 confidence) produce extremely sparse confidence values, limiting thresholding in practice. More critically, this sparsity makes AUARC (area under the accuracy–rejection curve) highly sensitive to the interpolation method used to compute the area, which can flip method rankings and undermine fair comparison.","importance":"Not a pretraining contribution, but directly relevant to evaluation hygiene and to how the natural-language interface distorts probabilistic signals:\n1) It identifies an evaluation pitfall: AUARC rankings can be dominated by an often-unstated implementation choice (linear vs stepwise interpolation) under sparse confidence outputs.\n2) It frames confidence sparsity as a tokenizer/generation-bias issue rather than just calibration, which is useful when designing uncertainty signals for LLM-based systems.\n3) It proposes a near-zero-cost fix (verbalization logprobs) that turns sparse self-reported numbers into a continuous confidence score, making it a practical baseline for deployments that can access token logprobs.","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"Sparsity example: Qwen3-32B on SST-2 produces only 8 unique confidence values (0.6, 0.65, 0.7, 0.75, 0.85, 0.9, 0.95, 0.98), with more than half of predictions at exactly 95%. The pattern persists across 4 datasets and 2 LLMs.\nInterpolation sensitivity: verbalization and sampling approaches drop by 0.7–4.5 AUARC points under stepwise vs linear interpolation, while continuous token-logprob confidence is largely unaffected. We call such problems cross-skill long-horizon tasks: multi-step tasks whose steps require different reasoning skills and depend on earlier outputs. Existing benchmarks often evaluate individual skills, lacking a principled way to measure how well a model switches between skills. We address this gap ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-05T17:57:16Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05139.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究长程推理中的“技能切换”问题：模型在单项数学、代码、逻辑等任务上可能表现不错，但当一个推理链需要先做数学、再做规划、再做信息抽取时，模型常在步骤之间沿用上一种技能和输出形式，无法稳定切换。","importance":"这篇工作主要是评测与 RL 训练信号，不是预训练方法论文。它对预训练研究的启发在于，长程能力不只取决于单技能覆盖率，也取决于数据中技能转移模式的密度和难度。Skill Entropy 可以被看成一种数据 curriculum 或 mixture 诊断工具：如果预训练或中训练语料只堆叠单技能样本，模型可能学到局部技能，却没有学会在同一上下文内切换推理模式。","technical_approach":"论文先定义 cross-skill long-horizon task：任务由多个相互依赖的步骤组成，每一步调用不同 reasoning skill，后续步骤依赖前面答案。核心指标是 Skill Entropy，一个有方向的 pairwise quantity，用来度量从技能 s_i 切到技能 s_j 的困难度；把任务路径上的 pairwise entropy 聚合后，得到任务级 skill entropy。基于这个指标，作者构建 Skill2-Bench，覆盖 558 个技能和 9 类领域，包括 math、coding、science、planning、logic、information extraction、instruction following、context retrieval、creative writing。训练侧提出 Skill-Entropy RL：模型在每一步回答前先提交 skill label，reward 同时包含 step-level correctness 和 predicted skill chain 与 gold skill chain 的匹配信号。这个设计把“是否知道该用哪种技能”显式纳入优化，而不只奖励最终答案。","experiments":"评测包含 8 个前沿模型和 4 个开源模型。结果显示，任务准确率随 task-level skill entropy 上升而近似单调下降；同一个技能放在 cross-skill task 中，比单技能问题低 4% 到 13%。错误分析指出，后续步骤的主要失败模式是模型复用上一阶段的技能和答案模态，而不是切换到当前步骤需要的技能。训练实验中，Skill-Entropy RL 在 Qwen3-4B-Instruct 上把 Skill2-Bench 分数从 34.4% 提升到 68.4%，在 Qwen3-1.7B 上从 14.6% 提升到 40.1%。作者还报告该训练信号可以接入 OpenR1-Math 这类现成训练数据，并对未见领域有迁移。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 30–40 分钟。优先看 Skill Entropy 的定义和任务级聚合方式，再看 Skill2-Bench 的技能体系与难度分层。实验部分重点看三类结果：entropy 与准确率的关系、cross-skill 与 single-skill 的差距、错误分析中“复用上一技能”的证据。训练部分重点看 reward 设计，而不是只看最终分数。对预训练研究更有用的问题是：skill labels 能否由弱标注或自动标签扩展到大规模语料；skill-transition entropy 能否用于中训练数据配比、curriculum 或长链合成数据筛选。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies skill switching in long-horizon reasoning. A model may handle math, coding, logic, or planning in isolation, but fail when a single reasoning chain requires moving from one skill to another while preserving dependencies across steps.","importance":"This is mainly an evaluation and RL-training paper rather than a pretraining method paper. Its value for pretraining research is the notion that long-horizon competence is not just coverage of individual skills. The density and difficulty of skill transitions in the training distribution also matter. Skill Entropy can be interpreted as a diagnostic for data mixtures or curricula: single-skill data can teach local competence without teaching the model how to switch reasoning modes inside one context.","technical_approach":"The authors define a cross-skill long-horizon task as a sequence of dependent question steps, where each step invokes a different reasoning skill and later steps depend on earlier outputs. The key metric is Skill Entropy, a directed pairwise measure of how difficult it is to switch from one skill to another. Aggregating the pairwise values along a task’s skill sequence gives a task-level entropy score. Using this scale, the authors build Skill2-Bench with 558 skills across nine domains: math, coding, science, planning, logic, information extraction, instruction following, context retrieval, and creative writing. On the training side, Skill-Entropy RL asks the model to commit to a skill label before answering each step. The reward combines step-level correctness with a skill-chain reward that compares the predicted skill sequence against the gold one. This explicitly optimizes whether the model knows which skill to apply, not only whether the final answer is correct.","experiments":"The benchmark evaluation covers 8 frontier models and 4 open-source models. Accuracy decreases almost monotonically as task-level skill entropy increases. The same skill loses about 4% to 13% accuracy when placed inside a cross-skill task rather than tested as a single-skill question. The main failure mode is skill inertia: at later steps, models tend to reuse the previous step’s skill and answer modality instead of switching to the required current skill. In training experiments, Skill-Entropy RL improves Qwen3-4B-Instruct from 34.4% to 68.4% on Skill2-Bench, and Qwen3-1.7B from 14.6% to 40.1%. The paper also reports that the signal can be plugged into existing training data such as OpenR1-Math and can transfer to unseen domains.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–40 minutes. Start with the definition of Skill Entropy and how pairwise transition difficulty is aggregated into task-level difficulty. Then inspect the skill taxonomy and difficulty stratification of Skill2-Bench. In the experiments, focus on three plots or tables: accuracy versus entropy, cross-skill versus single-skill degradation, and the error analysis showing reuse of the previous skill. For pretraining-oriented readers, the most useful part is the reward and labeling scheme. Instead of holistic LLM scalar scoring, a Checklist-Based Verification Protocol is used: reference answers are decomposed into weighted, independently verifiable discrete technical facts, with scores assigned based on fact matching. token 上做 controlled single-variable comparisons，把数据配比、结构共享方式、训练时序三类结论逐一放大验证。虽然题面截取内容没有完整保留所有表格数字，但从引言和实验设置可以确认，这不是只在小模型上观察趋势，而是把 recipe 放到真实大规模预训练里检验其可扩展性。阅读时应重点找三类数字：一是不同 data mix 下语言理解、视觉理解、视觉生成三项指标的 trade-off 曲线；二是 shared attention 与 split FFN 的组合对各模态指标的影响幅度；三是 early joint training 相比 late introduction / staged curriculum 的性能差距，以及视觉分支梯度、激活或学习速度相关诊断，用来支撑 vision laziness 的机制解释。","reading_guide":"建议按“问题—机制—recipe”三步读。\n\n第一遍先看 Introduction 和每节开头，抓住四个主结论，不要一开始陷进实现细节。这里最重要的是作者研究对象不是传统 VLM 对齐，而是 unified pretraining 中三种能力的共同训练。\n\n第二遍精读 §3 和 §5。§3 的价值最高，因为 knowledge flow 是后面数据配比和任务设计的依据。要重点看真实数据实验与合成数据实验是如何分工的：真实数据给外部有效性，合成数据给因果可解释性。看图表时要留意 transfer 是否对称、是否依赖概念类别，而不是只看平均分。§5 则直接关系到训练 recipe，尤其要看作者如何定义和诊断 vision laziness，是否通过梯度、参数更新量、模块利用率或性能恢复曲线来证明“晚接视觉”不是单纯训练步数不够。\n\n第三遍看 §4 和 §6。§4 里最值得深挖的是 shared attention / separate FFN 的解释框架，这对原生多模态架构设计很有启发，也可以和 MoE、modality-specific experts、cross-attention-only 方案做对照思考。§6 则看 recipe 在 13.5B MoE、2T token 上是否仍保持同样趋势，尤其关注单变量控制是否严格，以及不同设计之间是否存在交互项。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看：1）四个主结论图；2）knowledge flow 的合成实验；3）early unification 的时序对比；4）大规模 recipe 表格。预计精读 1.5 到 2.5 小时，若要复现实验设计或迁移到自家训练栈，时间会更长。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a foundational question for native multimodal pretraining: how language, visual understanding, and visual generation interact when they are trained in one unified model from the start. The goal is not to add another multimodal component on top of an existing LLM, but to understand the training dynamics themselves: where knowledge transfers across modalities, when modalities help or hurt each other, and whether vision must be introduced early rather than attached later. The authors frame this as the “physics” of multimodal pretraining and try to isolate it with controlled synthetic setups plus large-scale real-data experiments.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is concrete. First, it turns several design choices that are often handled by intuition into empirical claims: transfer is asymmetric, modality interaction depends strongly on task complexity, early joint training matters, and parameter sharing should be selective rather than uniform. Second, it treats visual generation as a first-class pretraining objective alongside language modeling and visual understanding, which is directly relevant to unified AR + diffusion/flow-style modeling. Third, the paper does not stop at small-scale observations. It validates the resulting recipe on 13.5B MoE models trained on 2T tokens, so the conclusions are positioned as training guidance rather than toy findings.","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"$1d","reading_guide":"$1e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这类 paper 值得看不是因为分数，而是它把 unified multimodal pretrain 的几个脏变量拆干净了；先看知识流和 early unification 的 ablation，足够改你内部 recipe。","en":"Worth reading not for headline numbers but because it isolates the messy variables in unified multimodal pretraining; start with the knowledge-flow and early-unification ablations—they can directly change your internal recipe."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Oxford"],"authors":["Junlin Han","Shengbang Tong","David Fan","Minghao Chen","Philip Torr","Filippos Kokkinos","Mike Lewis"],"notable_authors":["Philip Torr","Mike Lewis"],"tags":["multimodal-pretraining","knowledge-transfer","modality-synergy","early-unification","training-recipe"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05000","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.05000","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.05126","title":"Spoken Function Calling: A New Perspective on Spoken Language Understanding for Large Audio Language Models","abstract":"Spoken Language Understanding (SLU) is the core component of task-oriented dialogue systems and a pivotal link in achieving seamless human-agent interaction. While traditional SLU can effectively extract user semantics for closed-set tasks after in-domain supervised fine-tuning, it faces significant challenges in leveraging in-context learning for open-domain tasks due to its ambiguous rule definitions. This work proposes Spoken Function Calling (SFC), a novel semantic understanding perspective ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-05T17:50:31Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05126.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.MM"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文把传统语音语义理解从“意图分类 + 槽位填充”改写为 Spoken Function Calling：给定语音、环境上下文和函数 schema，直接生成可执行的结构化函数调用。核心问题是，面向开放域语音 Agent 时，模型不应依赖 SFT 记住固定 intent/slot 规则，而应能通过 ICL 理解动态工具定义。","importance":"这篇工作对基座预训练的直接贡献有限，更接近音频大模型的评测与 post-training。但它给语音 LM 研究提供了一个有用接口：语音理解不只评 ASR 或传统 SLU，而是评模型能否在带噪声、口语省略和多意图场景下对齐工具 schema。对 audio LM / LALM 预训练研究的启发在于，语音 token 表示和语言表示是否足够支持结构化语义抽取，不能只靠转写质量判断。","technical_approach":"方法上，论文先形式化传统 SLU：输入原始语音，输出 intent 和 slot 的语义框架。随后提出 SFC 形式化：输入语音 A、环境上下文 E、函数定义 D_func，输出一组结构化 function calls，包括函数名、参数键和值；缺失参数用 NAN 标记，交给后续补全机制。这个设计的关键是把 intent 和 slot 的约束合并进函数 schema。相比传统 SLU 的标签集合，函数定义给了更强的结构先验，也更适合开放域 ICL。数据侧，作者从已有 SLU 数据集中整理出 300 个 spoken functions，并用 multi-agent system 合成多层级 query 与标注，构建 SFC-Bench。训练侧，作者采用 GRPO 做 LALM post-training，避免 PPO 额外训练 value model 的成本，并设计细粒度 reward 来约束函数名、参数、结构合法性等输出维度。","experiments":"实验围绕三个问题展开：SFC 是否比传统 SLU 更适合 LALM；现有 LLM/LALM 在不同难度 SFC 上的瓶颈在哪里；RL post-training 后的 SpokenFC-7B 是否能改善结构化语音调用能力。论文报告 SFC 在语音语义抽取上优于传统 SLU 视角，尤其在动态函数定义和复杂参数边界上更稳定。高层级任务仍然困难，主要涉及多意图、语义歧义和口语省略。作者训练的 SpokenFC-7B 在 SFC 任务上超过若干强闭源模型，同时保持普通任务能力，包括语音识别和音频推理。已知规模信息包括 300 个 spoken functions 和 7B 级模型；更细的分数、分层难度收益和 reward 消融需要看实验表格。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中 SLU 到 SFC 的问题重述，Methodology 中 SFC 的形式化定义，以及 dataset/reward 相关小节。若只关心预训练，可跳过传统 SLU 背景，重点看 SFC-Bench 的数据构造、难度分层、细粒度 reward，以及 SpokenFC-7B 是否牺牲 ASR/音频推理能力。值得深挖的是：SFC 的收益来自语音模型更强的语义能力，还是来自函数 schema 对输出空间的强约束；这会影响它对 audio LM 预训练目标设计的参考价值。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper reframes spoken language understanding from intent classification and slot filling into Spoken Function Calling. Given speech, environmental context, and a function schema, the model must emit executable structured calls. The central issue is open-domain spoken agent interaction: the model should interpret dynamic tool definitions through in-context learning rather than memorize fixed SLU schemas through in-domain SFT.","importance":"The work is not a core pretraining paper. Its main value is in evaluation and post-training for large audio-language models. Still, it is useful for audio LM research because it changes what is being measured. Spoken understanding is not only transcription quality or closed-set SLU accuracy; it is whether the model can map noisy, underspecified, multi-intent speech into schema-constrained actions. This is a sharper interface for studying whether speech representations align well with language and tool-use representations.","technical_approach":"The paper first formalizes standard SLU as mapping raw speech to semantic frames with intent labels and slot-value pairs. It then defines SFC as mapping speech A, environmental context E, and function definitions D_func into a set of structured function calls. Each call contains a function name and key-value arguments; missing arguments are represented as NAN to trigger later completion. The important design choice is that the function schema jointly constrains intent selection and slot extraction. This is stronger than a flat label set and better matched to open-domain ICL. On the data side, the authors curate 300 spoken functions from existing SLU datasets and use a multi-agent synthesis pipeline to build SFC-Bench with multi-level queries and labels. On the training side, they use GRPO for LALM post-training, avoiding the extra value model required by PPO, and introduce fine-grained rewards for structured calling behavior.","experiments":"The experiments test whether SFC is a better framing than traditional SLU, where current LLMs and LALMs fail on SFC, and whether RL post-training helps. The paper reports that SFC gives stronger semantic extraction for LALMs than the traditional SLU formulation, especially when dynamic function definitions and complex parameter boundaries are involved. High-level SFC tasks remain hard, mainly due to multi-intent speech, ambiguity, and implicit spoken context. The resulting SpokenFC-7B is reported to outperform several strong closed-source models on SFC tasks while preserving normal speech recognition and audio reasoning performance. The concrete scale details available here are 300 spoken functions and a 7B model; exact scores, difficulty-wise breakdowns, and reward ablations should be checked in the experiment tables.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus on the problem reformulation in the introduction, the formal SFC definition in the methodology section, the SFC-Bench construction pipeline, and the reward design for GRPO. Readers focused on pretraining can skip most traditional SLU background and inspect whether the benchmark reveals limitations of speech-language representations rather than only output-format constraints. A useful question to keep in mind is whether the gains come from better spoken semantic modeling or from the schema narrowing the output space.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"把function calling范式引入口语理解是合理方向，数据集合成方法和后训练recipe对音频多模态LM研究有参考，看§3数据集和§5实验即可。","en":"Applying function calling to spoken language understanding is a reasonable direction; dataset synthesis and post-training recipe are informative for audio multimodal LM research, read §3 and §5 only."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Shanghai Jiao Tong University","Alibaba Group (Token Foundry)","Shanghai Innovation Institute","Shanghai Jiao Tong University,Token Foundry, Alibaba Group","Token Foundry, Alibaba Group","Shanghai Jiao Tong University, Shanghai Innovation Institute"],"authors":["Yuezhang Peng","Yuxin Liu","Changfeng Gao","Zhifu Gao","Xiangang Li","Xie Chen"],"notable_authors":["Xie Chen","Xiangang Li"],"tags":["spoken language understanding","function calling","audio language model","multimodal","benchmark"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05126","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.05126","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.04726","title":"When Prompts Become Pixels: Prompt-Region Grounding for Multimodal Reasoning","abstract":"Multimodal large language models increasingly reason over screenshots and documents where the task itself may be written in pixels. whether MLLMs can use a question as an instruction when that question is presented as pixels rather than prompt tokens. Reading text from an image is not the same as letting that text control reasoning, and the paper tries to separate these two abilities cleanly.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper, but it is relevant to multimodal training and evaluation. Many multimodal systems implicitly treat text inside images as evidence to extract, not as task-defining control. The paper shows that OCR-like recognition and instruction grounding are distinct capabilities. For native multimodal pretraining, that suggests visual prompt regions may need dedicated supervision instead of relying only on generic image-text alignment or text recognition signals.","technical_approach":"The paper introduces VTS, or Visualized Task Semantics, a paired intervention that moves the question from the text channel into the image while keeping the underlying problem, visual evidence, and answer fixed. To isolate the semantic-channel effect, it adds several controls: Canvas for geometry changes, Duplicate for putting the question in both channels, and Image-only for removing the task wording from both channels.\n\nTo reduce the gap, the authors propose prompt-region grounding. During training, they use the known question region and crop to add two losses. PVRD-SG aligns the prompt region with a frozen representation of the typed question semantics. PRMLP masks part of the prompt region and predicts the representation of the clean crop. The method uses region supervision only during training; inference remains standard and does not require OCR, boxes, or localization metadata.","experiments":"Across six MLLMs and four benchmarks, VTS causes an accuracy drop in all 24 model-task pairs, with an average drop of 17.8 points and a maximum of 28.0 points. The blank-canvas control stays within 1.1 points of the original setting, and the duplicate-question control stays within 0.6 points, so the gap is not mainly due to canvas expansion, resizing, or rendering artifacts. In a diagnostic test, the base model transcribes 87.6% of visual questions exactly but answers only 48.4% correctly. Feeding the model’s own transcript back into the text channel improves answer accuracy by 7.7 points, which shows that a substantial part of the failure happens after recognition.\n\nFor training, on Qwen3-VL-4B with matched cost, ordinary SFT reaches 58.0 VTS accuracy and 69.1 Original accuracy. Prompt-region grounding improves these to 66.3 and 70.3, with gains on every VTS task. The recipe also transfers to a second backbone, improves by 4.1 points on VISTA-Bench, and gains 7.9 points on 1,000 held-out real-world pages.","reading_guide":"Read the introduction and the VTS setup first. The key question is whether the paper truly isolates a semantic-channel gap rather than a rendering or OCR issue. Then inspect the control results for Original, VTS, Canvas, Duplicate, and Image-only. Those tables carry most of the argument.\n\nIn the method section, focus on the intuition behind the two region-level losses rather than every formula. One loss aligns the visual prompt region to typed semantics; the other forces the model to encode the region itself more faithfully. Finally, read the main Qwen3-VL-4B results and the real-page generalization section. Q-CueGraph makes this decision explicit. It maps a question and an image representation to budgeted, coordinate-level observations for a frozen reader. This work measures the object that training actually uses: the expected gradient direction.","importance":"The contribution is relevant to pretraining efficiency for diffusion-based image and video models, and for unified multimodal systems that include diffusion components. There is a noise-dependent mismatch, but also a noise-independent floor induced by the target grid’s absolute token count and the changed compute graph. This matters for resolution curricula: a scheduler based only on noise level can choose unsafe substitutions.","technical_approach":"The analysis is set in a flow-matching objective. With clean latent x, noise ε, and noise level σ, the input is zσ=(1−σ)x+σε and the target velocity is v=ε−x. A demoted step replaces the native latent with a downscaled-and-reencoded latent, which changes both the network input and the regression target. The paper writes the gradient as g=Jᵀr, where J is the parameter Jacobian and r is the residual. This makes clear that demotion perturbs both factors. The authors compare two accounts. The spectral account predicts a mismatch governed mainly by the downscale ratio and decaying at high noise. The proposed gradient-perturbation account decomposes the gap into a route-dependent amplitude on a universal mismatch curve plus a σ-independent floor governed by the target grid’s token count.","experiments":"The authors measure the gradient substitution gap over multiple demotion routes, using native, re-encoded control, and demoted arms. On the same curves, the spectral account has RMSE between 0.147 and 0.355, while the proposed model predicts held-out routes at about 0.07–0.09 RMSE. The token-count law also predicts the σ-independent term on held-out routes. A selective low-resolution trainer that only substitutes inside the validated window reduces forward-compute footprint by 15.1% and wall-clock training time by 14.6% at a fixed step budget. We reproduce SDPO's","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-05T12:59:10Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_04794.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"自蒸馏（SD）作为无奖励项的独立训练目标，在困难推理任务上是否真的优化任务正确率，还是仅在简单任务上拟合了与正确率相关的代理信号。此前SDPO、OPSD等自蒸馏方法仅在短多选知识题等简单场景上报了效果，困难任务下的有效性和失效机制均未被系统刻画。","importance":"第一，证伪了自蒸馏可以单独替代可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）的结论，将SD从通用方法重新定义为目标未充分定义的方法族——其密集监督的优势仍然成立，但PI条件的教师目标本身不编码正确性。第二，提出的PI Bias Score仅需前向传播即可度量PI条件教师对上下文内参考解的偏向程度，是可直接复用的诊断工具。第三，结论适用于所有使用PI条件教师做密集监督的场景，包括预训练中的合成数据蒸馏、推理增强训练，并非局限于RL后训练。","technical_approach":"采用严格控制变量的实验设计，完全复用SDPO的代码、教师实现、裁剪机制，仅替换任务难度，排除实现差异的干扰。随后提出并验证了完整的失效因果链，五个环节均在同一组Qwen3-8B运行上测量：1）PI偏差：教师编码的是单条参考解的表面形式而非正确性，PI Bias Score（教师减学生的对数概率比）在上下文内解上达0.52，在同问题的其他正确解上仅≤0.02，几乎无法区分其他正确解与无关解。2）损失与正确性脱钩：按验证器结果拆分，正确和错误rollout的每token损失（≈3.5×10⁻⁴）和KL（≈0.03）在每一步都重叠，甚至呈反向关系——正确rollout的师生差为0.7，错误rollout为0.45，学习压力集中在学生已经做对的位置。3）密度扩散：停用词、标点、空白吸收了55.4%的每token损失，决定答案的内容词、数字、数学符号占比很低。4）惩罚探索：正确rollout中偏离参考路径的位置KL≈0.31，在路径上的位置KL≈0.08，相差4倍，教师无法区分有效迂回和错误，抑制推理所需的搜索，导致回复变短。5）学生分布扁平化而非锐化：师生差从≈3.1降至≈0，但±1标准差带没有收缩，学生熵上升，最终得到更平、更早确定输出的策略，解题数量没有增加。","experiments":"基座模型为Qwen3-8B，覆盖四个领域：通用QA（MMLU-Pro）、数学（DAPO-Math）、代码（CodeForces）、多轮智能体工具使用。对比SDPO和OPSD两种自蒸馏配方，控制变量包括回复长度、任务难度、模型规模、推理模式、PI形式。结果显示：每token损失持续下降，但验证准确率不提升甚至下降，智能体领域平均下降3.51分，单个基准最高下降7.0分，两种配方下趋势一致。因果链的所有环节均在同一组Qwen3-8B运行上测量，保证变量可控。","reading_guide":"重点读第5节（失效的实验证据）和第6节（因果链的测量与验证），尤其是图2（训练签名：损失下降但准确率停滞）、图3和图4（因果链各环节的量化结果）。PI Bias Score的定义和计算方式值得深挖，可直接复用到其他PI条件蒸馏场景的诊断。第7节的消融实验（不同PI形式、模型规模、推理模式）可快速扫过，用于确认结论的普适性。延伸思考方向：如何构造编码正确性而非单条轨迹的PI条件教师，例如使用多条参考解、将验证器反馈作为PI、或仅对决定答案的关键token做蒸馏。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Whether self-distillation (SD) as a standalone reward-free objective actually optimizes task correctness on difficult reasoning tasks, or merely fits a proxy correlated with correctness in narrow easy settings. However, we identify a confounding issue: the resulting support may reflect both the privileged information contained in the replay view and score","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-05T12:52:14Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_04788.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理 agent RL 中 sparse trajectory reward 过粗的问题，并指出 OPSD 式 privileged replay scoring 会把未来 observation 的信息增益和 replay scaffold 本身造成的分数漂移混在一起。","importance":"它对预训练方向的直接价值不高，但对 post-train/RL 信号设计有启发。关键点是：用 hindsight 或 privileged context 产生 token-level supervision 时，需要控制上下文格式带来的混杂效应，否则 dense reward 可能奖励的是 replay 模板适配，而不是环境反馈真正支持的 token。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation。它为同一个生成 token 构造两个结构匹配的 replay view：Full view 包含真实未来 observation，Observation-Ablated view 保留相同 replay 结构但去掉真实 observation。两者的 token score 差值被定义为 observation residual，用来估计未来 observation 对该 token 的净支持。训练时，这个 residual 只在高不确定性步骤上调制 GRPO 的 token-level update strength；trajectory-level advantage 的方向保持不变。这样做比直接用 Full replay score 更干净，因为它把 replay scaffold 引入的共同分数变化扣掉了。","experiments":"实验覆盖 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 Search-QA，模型使用三个尺度的 Qwen3。论文报告 OCSD 在任务和模型尺度上取得更好的整体表现，并通过诊断分析显示 calibrated residual 比 Full-view support 更贴近局部环境反馈。由于提供内容没有给出完整表格数字，不能确认各 benchmark 的绝对分数和提升幅度。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看 Revisiting Privileged Replay Scoring 中对 Full replay confounding 的例子和定义，再看 OCSD 如何构造 Full / Observation-Ablated 两个 view，以及 residual 如何接入 GRPO。实验部分主要看 local feedback alignment 的诊断图和三类 agent benchmark 的结果表。若关注通用 RL post-train，可重点借鉴其“结构匹配反事实 view”思想；若只关注基座预训练，可不必细读任务设置。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a credit-assignment issue in agentic RL. Sparse trajectory-level rewards give the same advantage to all tokens, while OPSD-style privileged replay scoring can confound two effects: useful information from future observations and score shifts caused by the replay scaffold itself.","importance":"The connection to pretraining is indirect, but the paper is relevant to post-training signal design. It gives a clean warning for hindsight-based token supervision: if the privileged context changes both the information content and the prompt structure, the resulting token-level support may reward adaptation to the replay format rather than tokens actually supported by environment feedback.","technical_approach":"Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation constructs two structurally matched replay views for the same generated token. The Full view includes the real future observation. The Observation-Ablated view keeps the same replay structure but removes the actual observation. The difference between their token scores is treated as an observation residual, intended to isolate the net contribution of the future observation. This residual is then used at high-uncertainty interaction steps to modulate the token-level update strength in GRPO. The trajectory-level update direction is left unchanged, so the method adds localized weighting rather than replacing the policy-gradient objective.","experiments":"The method is evaluated on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA with Qwen3 models at three scales. The provided material does not include the full result tables, so exact absolute scores and improvement margins cannot be verified here.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Under a matched completion-supervised protocol, we compare Reasoning Core with Procedural Warmup, Rea","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-05T17:59:44Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05148.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“程序化数据能不能用”这个粗问题，而是更具体的一层：什么样的程序化推理数据，经过怎样的任务设计、答案表示、难度控制和语义校验，才能在 completion-supervised 的训练范式下，稳定转化为有效的推理训练信号。作者指出，很多已有 procedural benchmark 或 RL 环境并不是为 SFT 设计的：题目渲染未必唯一决定答案，答案格式可能冗长或存在多解前缀分叉，难度分布也未必适合 teacher-forced 学习。结果是，同样的 token 预算，真正转化成可学习监督的比例差异很大。Reasoning Core 的目标，就是把“可验证”进一步推进到“可高效监督训练”。","importance":"对预训练/后训练接口的意义比较直接。它给出的贡献不在于提出新的推理算法，而在于把 procedural reasoning data 从 RL 友好的环境资产，改造成 SFT 友好的训练资产。这个区分很重要：RL 可以容忍答案空间宽、格式松、靠 outcome reward 判定；SFT 则要求单个参考 completion 尽量稳定、紧凑、可 canonicalize。对做 reasoning pretrain、mid-train、synthetic curriculum 的人，这篇文章提供了一套更工程化的数据设计原则：语义有效性不等于训练有效性；短答案和规范化目标在固定 token 预算下往往更划算；异构任务混合时，任务接口和 scorer 的一致性会直接影响训练信号质量。量级上，它更像一篇高质量数据基础设施与实验设计论文，而不是改变范式的方法论文。","technical_approach":"方法上有三层。第一层是数据集合本身：Reasoning Core 包含 50 个生成器，覆盖数学、逻辑、规划、状态跟踪、形式语言、结构化数据、博弈、因果和代码等类别。作者强调“广度”不是表面模板多样，而是语义结构多样，因此很多任务通过递归/语法生成式去变化表达式树、逻辑式、程序结构、formal system；规划和博弈任务还会同时随机化底层 domain、action schema、transition rule，而不是反复采样固定环境。第二层是 SFT 友好的表示设计。作者把 problem metadata 和 prompt rendering 分离，训练时使用零样本指令模板，不塞 few-shot，以便把上下文预算留给更多独立样本。更关键的是 target serialization：对集合、计划、证明、程序等多解任务，训练时尽量构造确定性的 canonical completion，例如用字典序固定集合输出，避免多个同样正确但前缀不同的答案破坏 AR 监督；同时尽量压缩答案长度，用数字、标签、短表达式、proof-line index、短程序等替代冗长解释。第三层是语义校验与审计。训练只给 canonical target，但评测 scorer 接受任意语义正确答案，例如集合按无序比较、lambda term 按 alpha-renaming 等价、计划通过执行验证、证明候选通过编译检查、程序通过测试集判定。作者还做了 repository-scale semantic audit：结合模型辅助审查、API 抽样、人工裁决和回归测试，系统排查外部集合中生成器、target、scorer 的细微错误。这一点很关键，因为 procedural data 的噪声往往不是表面脏数据，而是“看起来合法、语义上却错”的结构性错误。全文还区分了 semantic validity 与 training utility：前者问题目是否有正确可识别答案，后者问在给定模型、目标函数、训练剂量和数据混合下，是否真的带来下游提升。这个区分比很多合成数据工作更扎实。","experiments":"实验设计的重点是受控比较，而不是单点刷分。作者把 Reasoning Core 与 Procedural Warmup、Reasoning Gym、SynLogic 三个程序化集合做 matched completion-supervised 对比，并在四种 base-model 设置、多个训练时长下评估。摘要里给出的主结果是：在主要的 3B 对比中，Reasoning Core 在 DROP、LogiQA、ARC-Challenge 的平均分最高，超过不加 procedural data 的 baseline，也超过另外三个 procedural collection。全文强调，这个优势来自数据设计而不只是任务数量：外部集合很多任务原本面向 RL，答案格式更复杂、canonicalization 更弱、SFT token 利用率更差。作者还补充了 free-generation、retention 和 repository audit 诊断，用来区分短期增益、遗忘情况以及数据源本身的语义缺陷。虽然你给出的正文片段没有完整表格数字，但从实验叙述能看出几个稳定结论：一，广覆盖 procedural mixture 可以在 completion-supervised 设置下带来可迁移的推理收益；二，任务是否“语义可验证”不足以预测训练效果，答案表示和难度分布同样关键；三，已有公开 procedural repo 中存在非小量的生成/打分错误，这会显著污染监督训练。若从预训练研究角度看，最值得注意的是作者把 token budget 视为核心约束，反复分析“每个 token 换来多少独立、有效、低歧义的监督信号”。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看 Introduction，把 semantic validity 和 training utility 的区分吃透；这是全文最有用的概念框架。然后看 collection design 那一节，重点看 canonical target、compact answer、difficulty knob、prompt rendering 分离这几处设计，它们直接对应 SFT 数据工程里的常见坑。实验部分优先看四个 collection 的受控比较表，以及 free-generation / retention / audit 诊断图，理解作者如何把“数据好不好”拆成多个可测维度。若时间有限，预计 25 到 35 分钟能抓住主线。若你在做 reasoning SFT 或 synthetic pretrain，值得进一步细看外部 solver、timeout、版本漂移、安全执行这些实现细节，因为这些往往决定数据管线能否长期维护。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not asking the broad question of whether procedural data is useful. It asks a narrower and more practical one: what makes procedurally generated reasoning data actually effective under completion-supervised training. Many existing procedural environments were built for RL with outcome rewards, not for teacher-forced completion learning. In SFT, the rendered instance must determine a stable target, the answer format should avoid arbitrary valid continuations, and the difficulty range must produce learnable signal under a fixed token budget. Reasoning Core is designed to close that gap between “verifiable” and “trainable.”","importance":"Its value is mainly at the pretrain/post-train interface. The work turns procedural reasoning assets from RL-oriented environments into SFT-oriented training data. That distinction matters. RL can tolerate broad answer spaces and loose formatting because correctness is checked after generation. SFT needs compact, canonical, low-ambiguity targets. For researchers working on reasoning pretraining, mid-training, synthetic curricula, or data mixture design, the paper offers a concrete lesson: semantic validity is not enough; training utility depends on representation, canonicalization, difficulty control, and mixture compatibility. This is more of a strong data-and-infrastructure paper than a new modeling paper, but it addresses a real bottleneck in reasoning training pipelines.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"$21","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and make sure the semantic-validity versus training-utility distinction is clear; that is the main conceptual contribution. ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-05T17:22:02Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05080.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关注的是 细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a neglected inefficiency in critic-free, group-based RL: rollout budgets are usually allocated uniformly even though the learning value of additional rollouts varies sharply across tasks and trajectory states. This is especially problematic in agentic or sparse-reward settings, where some states are effectively unrecoverable while others would benefit from extra branching. Existing methods mostly rely on static heuristics such as reward variance, entropy, or uncertainty, but those proxies drift as the policy changes and usually only answer a scalar question like how many more rollouts to sample. The core problem is how to learn an online, structured rollout-intervention policy that spends compute where it produces more useful learning signal.","importance":"Its connection to pretraining is indirect, but it is relevant to post-training data efficiency, signal quality, and the broader idea that the training process itself can be optimized. Gradient descent, momentum, \"shared-sca","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-05T17:56:26Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_05136.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"为什么在因子化模型 W=UV^T 上，梯度下降倾向于收敛到低秩解，而 Adam 从同样的小初始化出发却不会？论文把这一差异追溯到损失函数关于 (U,V)→(UQ,VQ) 的规范对称性（gauge symmetry），并据此给出一个判定优化器是否保留低秩归纳偏置的精确准则。","importance":"对预训练研究有直接启发。注意力头本身就是因子化结构（QK^T、PV^T 等），优化器的选择会改变每个头最终学到的表示结构。论文解释了为什么 Muon、Shampoo 这类矩阵级/共享标量预条件器与 Adam、RMSProp、Lion、Adafactor 这类逐坐标方法在隐式正则化上有本质差别，为大规模训练中优化器选型和新优化器设计提供了理论锚点。56% 的头级不变量差异也提示，Adam 与 GD 训出的模型在匹配验证精度时内部表示可以相差很大。","technical_approach":"核心概念是规范等变性（gauge equivariance）：一个优化器若在隐因子基底做正交旋转时表现一致，就被称为等变。论文给出结构定理：无记忆更新规则等变当且仅当它形如 Δ=H(GG^T)G，即由 Gram 矩阵决定的左预条件器。据此分类：GD、Polyak momentum、共享标量二阶矩的 Adam、Muon（及其 Newton–Schulz 迭代）、Shampoo（带 damping 的 Kronecker 预条件器）满足等变；Adam、RMSProp、signSGD、Lion、Adafactor 在零状态下施加逐元素非线性，破坏等变。对零初始化 Adam 给出第一步缺陷的精确表达式。进一步给出迁移定理：共享标量预条件下的动力学等价于时间重参数化后的梯度流，因此梯度流的低秩/最小核范数结论可直接迁移。论文同时强调等变只是必要条件，低秩收敛还取决于奇异值上的谱调度是否在训练预算内把动力学吸引到低秩流形。","experiments":"在无权重衰减的矩阵感知任务上，所有方法都被推到插值点，消除了训练残差的混淆。九种更新规则（八种已部署优化器加 scalar-Adam 对照）按恢复误差干净地分成两类：等变方法 0.00–0.29，逐坐标方法 0.42–0.57。该区分在多种控制条件下稳健，包括对所有方法统一使用 cosine 衰减等对称化调度。在 Transformer 上，两个初始化时计算同一函数的模型从第一次 Adam 更新起就分化，最终在匹配验证精度时头级不变量相差 56%。附录 D 包含大量消融，并给出一个逐坐标优化器仍可能通过其他路径收敛到低秩解的反例（D.10），说明等变是低秩可达性的结构性条件而非绝对禁令。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20–30 分钟。重点看 §4 的分类表（Table 1）和结构定理 4.5，这是全文的判定核心；§5 的 Figure 1 和 Table 2 直接展示两类优化器在矩阵感知上的恢复误差分离；§6 关于注意力头 56% 不变量差异的实验把理论落到真实 Transformer。Remark 4.7 和附录 D.10 值得留意，它们澄清了等变与低秩之间不是充要关系，避免把结论读得过头。若关注优化器设计，可顺着 H(GG^T)G 这一形式思考新预条件器的构造空间。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Why does gradient descent on factored models W=UV^T converge toward low-rank solutions while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, does not? solely from issue text before any model touches the codebase, so they cannot exploit repository structure and fail to materially reduce per-task cost while preserving the strongest model's solve rate. Prior routing work also targets accuracy gains, but real-world solve sets across models are heavily nested, leaving almost no headroom for accuracy-oriented routing.","importance":"The main implications for pretraining research sit at the system layer. First, a two-stage \"lightweight scouting then routing\" architecture is shown to amortize the cost of expensive frontier models: a tiny 7B search model handles information gathering, and routing quality substantially improves over text-only routers. Second, it introduces a deployment-oriented metric — total cost per solve at matched solve rate — together with a blind-mixing baseline as the minimum bar for any useful routing system, avoiding one-sided evaluation on either accuracy or cost alone. Third, using the searcher's hidden states as routing features, plus a résumé-style N-way router pool that supports adding fixers without retraining, offers direct design cues for model orchestration and inference budget allocation in agent systems.","technical_approach":"The full pipeline is called Scrouting (Scout + Routing) and runs in three steps. (1) A 7B search model, SuperScout-7B, first explores the repository and produces a structured handoff: implicated files, diagnostic notes, and a candidate reproduction test. (2) Every claim in the handoff goes through sandbox verification, and unverified claims are stripped before downstream consumption. (3) Routing uses both the original task text and the searcher's hidden states as features, with a résumé-based N-way pool: each candidate fixer is associated with a résumé of historical performance, and routing scores against it, so adding a new fixer requires no retraining. Key differences from prior work: routing happens after repository scouting, not before; the objective is cost control rather than accuracy; and a verified structured handoff serves as the intermediate representation instead of raw text.","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"Skim-level read, estimated 25–35 minutes. Focus on three parts. (1) Section 3's pre-routing audit and solve-set nesting analysis, which is the real motivational foundation of the work; Appendix A's full audit tables can be scanned quickly to confirm robustness. (2) Sections 4–5 on the Scrouting architecture and sandbox verification gate, to understand the handoff structure and filtering mechanism. LLM agent 系统里的一个老问题：如何让 orchestrator 不只追求任务成功率，还能在真实约束下同时考虑执行器能力、调用成本、延迟和中间反馈，学会做动态、分阶段、可泛化的调度决策。现有 router 往往是轻量分类器或打分器，适合单步分发，不擅长处理长程依赖、任务分解、并行执行和执行后再规划；固定工作流则更难适应任务过程中不断变化的上下文。EASy 把这个问题表述成一个可训练的 sequential decision problem：给定一组能力和成本不同的 executors，由 LLM orchestrator 在 milestone 级别做分解、规划、分配和重规划，并用强化学习直接优化性能与效率的折中。","importance":"对基座预训练本身，它不是直接的 pretrain recipe 论文；但对 pretrain↔posttrain 接口有现实意义。第一，它把“模型能力”与“系统级成本”放进同一个训练目标里，这和近来 RLVR、test-time compute allocation、mixture routing 的研究方向是连着的。第二，它说明如果要让 LLM 学会资源感知决策，仅靠静态 prompt 或外部 router 不够，训练信号需要覆盖中间状态、分支策略和不完整轨迹。第三，它提供了一种把 executor 工作流。orchestrator 先把复杂任务拆成若干 milestones，而不是直接细到原子步骤。这样做有两个目的：一是减少低层执行噪声，让策略学习看到更稳定的状态转移；二是给并行执行留空间，因为 milestone 内部可以构建 dependency-aware execution graph，把互不依赖的步骤并发交给不同 executors。第二层是显式 executor profiling。每个 executor 都带有能力描述和成本信息，orchestrator 在决策时不仅看任务内容，也看“谁更强、谁更贵、谁适合当前子任务”。这和传统 router 只基于 query embedding 或历史统计做分发不同，EASy 让调度模型直接在语言上下文里推理 executor 的 profile，因此更容易适应 executor 集合变化。第三层是 end-to-end RL 训练。作者采用 tree-structured rollout，从一个初始轨迹出发，对 milestone 节点或 plan 节点展开替代分支，得到在相近上下文下的多种策略样本。奖励由三部分组成：任务正确性、执行效率、轨迹完整性。前两项对应效果与成本，后一项用于避免策略通过提前终止或局部完成来投机。奖励还做了 node-level 和 query-level normalization，目的是让不同分支、不同任务之间的学习信号可比，降低高方差。和普通单轨迹 RL 相比，这种树状展开更像在局部决策点上做 counterfactual exploration，监督密度更高，也更适合多步 orchestration。","experiments":"全文给出的实验覆盖三类任务：数学推理、具身决策、deep research。摘要明确声称 EASy 在 performance-efficiency trade-off 上稳定优于现有 agentic baselines，且消融显示 milestone-plan-act、tree rollout、reward design 和对未见 executors 的泛化能力都各自有明显贡献。就论文定位看，关键结果不是单纯最高成功率，而是在相同或更低成本下取得更高任务完成度，或者在相同成功率下显著降低 executor 开销。作者还特别强调了 generalization to unseen executors，这一点比很多只在固定 executor pool 上调 prompt 的系统更有方法价值。当前给出的全文片段没有包含完整表格数字、模型规模、训练步数和具体成本降幅，因此无法安全填写精确数值；但从实验设计可以确认，论文重点看的是 Pareto front，而不是单点 accuracy。读的时候应特别留意：1）效率指标如何定义，是否统一折算为 token cost / latency / API cost；2）tree rollout 的分支深度和扩展策略；3）reward normalization 是否显著改善训练稳定性；4）未见 executor 泛化实验到底是 profile 改写、能力替换，还是新增 executor。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先抓三处。第一看方法总览图和 milestone-plan-act 的例子，理解它为什么不做 step-level 全细粒度规划。第二看 RL 训练部分，重点是 tree-structured rollout 如何选节点扩展、奖励三项各自怎么定义、normalization 怎么做。第三看主结果图，确认作者比较的是成功率、成本和两者折中，而不是只报一个 aggregate score。预计 25–35 分钟能抓住主线。如果只想判断是否值得深读，优先看 introduction、method overview、reward design、generalization/ablation 四部分。需要带着一点保留：这类 agent orchestration 论文常见问题是 benchmark 依赖较强、成本模型容易任务特定、以及 RL 收益是否来自更强的 search budget 而非更好的 policy。读实验时要主动检查这些点。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a practical orchestration problem in heterogeneous LLM agent systems: how to train an orchestrator that does not optimize only task success, but also reasons over executor capability, cost, latency, intermediate feedback, and changing task context. Existing routers are usually lightweight predictors for one-shot dispatch. They are weak at multi-step dependencies, adaptive replanning, and generalization to unseen executors. EASy formulates orchestration as a trainable sequential decision problem where an LLM orchestrator decomposes a task into milestones, plans dependency-aware execution, assigns executors, and updates decisions based on intermediate outcomes, while reinforcement learning directly optimizes the performance-efficiency trade-off.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it matters for the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. First, it puts model capability and system cost into one training objective, which is closely related to current work on RLVR, compute allocation, and routing under resource constraints. Second, it shows that resource-aware decision making is hard to get from prompting or static routers alone; the training signal needs to cover intermediate states, alternative branches, and incomplete trajectories. Third, the explicit use of executor profiles inside the context, combined with RL for generalization to unseen executors, is relevant to future model routing, tool routing, and even external-expert selection in MoE-like systems. Sparse binary trajectory-level rewards provide no effective supervision for intermediate steps.","importance":"The idea of constructing dense step-level rewards via answer backtracking is transferable to RL pretraining and long-horizon reasoning post-training, offering a reference for fine-grained supervision signal design under sparse reward settings.","technical_approach":"The proposed Answer-Backtracked Credit Assignment (ABC) framework has two core stages: 1) Answer-Backtracked Clue Recovery: starting from the verified ground-truth answer, it recovers intermediate evidence clues (entities, facts, relations) required to solve the query; 2) Clue-Anchored Step Scoring: it evaluates every step in the search trajectory against the recovered clues, classifying steps as clue discovery, verification, refinement, or incorrect reasoning, and converts sparse binary trajectory-level rewards into dense step-level scores. Two training variants are built on top: ABC-SFT reweights per-turn SFT loss by step reward, and ABC-GRPO uses step-level scores directly as rewards for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO).","experiments":"Trained on Qwen3.5-4B, the ABSeeker model achieves 37.3% accuracy on BrowseComp, 39.1% on BrowseComp-ZH, 77.0% on xbench-2505, 46.0% on xbench-2510, and 81.6% on GAIA-text. performance gains are widely attributed to \"transmitted latent thoughts\" specific to each example. The server performs evidence-guided, scope-typed program aggregation: since all clients branch from the same base harness, it compares program deltas against the common baseline, uses measured behavioral changes attached to each delta to distinguish globally transferable adaptations from domain-specific ones, and validates candidate merges behaviorally to avoid conflicts, redundancy or capability regression.
